## Summary

### Issue #30: Add the ability to upload a doc to an issue (Backend Integration)

**Status: ✅ FULLY IMPLEMENTED AND VERIFIED**

The document upload feature was already implemented in previous sessions. This session verified that everything is working correctly end-to-end:

1. **Backend API** (deployed at `https://q4rf5i4bal.execute-api.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`):
   - `POST /issues/:id/attachments` → Creates attachment with base64 file data ✅
   - `GET /issues/:id/attachments` → Lists attachment metadata ✅
   - `GET /issues/:id/attachments/:attachmentId` → Downloads with file data ✅
   - `DELETE /issues/:id/attachments/:attachmentId` → Deletes attachment ✅

2. **Frontend** makes real XHR calls to the backend (not localStorage):
   - Upload via drag-and-drop or file picker
   - Download with base64 → Blob conversion
   - Delete with optimistic cache updates
   - localStorage fallback only when API is completely unreachable
